Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the modules framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 456. The resulting operator achieves bound O(173/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by modules generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 456, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 173. QED.
